Entry 1
Category: Programming Fundamentals
Concept: Programming
Definition: Programming is the process of writing instructions that a computer can execute.
Key Points:
- Frequently used in software development.
- Important for coding interviews.
- Applicable across multiple programming languages.
Example:
Programming is commonly used while developing software.
Related Concepts:
Algorithms, Data Structures, OOP, Software Engineering, Debugging
